# Acceptance Criteria - jupyterlab_workspace_manager_extension

The workspace panel that replaces the built-in Running panel: one accordion section per JupyterLab workspace with its tabs, terminals and kernels. Design in `docs/design/workspace-manager-design.html`, brief in `docs/brief.md`.

## Authors

- `@kj` Konrad Jelen

## Panel `PANEL`

The workspace panel in place of the built-in Running panel, its header and its reloads

- [x] `ACC-PANEL-1-REPLACE` **Replaces the built-in panel** - CRITICAL; plugin `@jupyterlab/running-extension:sidebar` is disabled; the workspace panel holds the sidebar place of Running Terminals and Kernels
  - evidence: 1 test(s) in Galata panel.spec.ts passed in full Galata rig run of final-verify (galata-full.log and galata-full.json, 91 of 91 passed, 6 workers, --retries=0)
  - test: Galata panel.spec.ts: load lab, assert one sidebar tab `jp-running-sessions` showing the workspace panel and no `.jp-RunningSessions` section list
  - test-tags: FUNCTIONAL
  - log: 2026-10-23T13:58:15Z @kj added
  - log: 2026-10-24T10:03:58Z @kj closed; reason: final verification of the implementation, 2026-10-24
- [x] `ACC-PANEL-2-RESTORE` **Saved layouts restore the panel** - HIGH; a saved layout whose left sidebar showed the Running panel opens the workspace panel in the same place; restorer name stays `running-sessions`
  - evidence: 1 test(s) in Galata panel.spec.ts passed in full Galata rig run of final-verify (galata-full.log and galata-full.json, 91 of 91 passed, 6 workers, --retries=0)
  - test: Galata panel.spec.ts: save layout with left current `running-sessions`, reload, assert workspace panel visible
  - test-tags: FUNCTIONAL
  - log: 2026-10-23T13:58:15Z @kj added
  - log: 2026-10-24T10:03:58Z @kj closed; reason: final verification of the implementation, 2026-10-24
- [x] `ACC-PANEL-3-SHOWCMD` **Show panel command** - MEDIUM; command `running:show-panel` exists and shows the workspace panel
  - evidence: 1 test(s) in Galata panel.spec.ts passed in full Galata rig run of final-verify (galata-full.log and galata-full.json, 91 of 91 passed, 6 workers, --retries=0)
  - test: Galata panel.spec.ts: hide the sidebar, execute `running:show-panel`, assert panel visible
  - test-tags: FUNCTIONAL
  - log: 2026-10-23T13:58:15Z @kj added
  - log: 2026-10-24T10:03:58Z @kj closed; reason: final verification of the implementation, 2026-10-24

- [x] `ACC-PANEL-8-POLL` **Reload while visible** - MEDIUM; while the panel is visible it reloads every 10 s, without the turning icon; a reload, by the poll or the refresh button, keeps the open section and its scroll position, the keyboard focus, the open cards and an open menu; a reload keeps the selection while the row stays lit under DQ-32; the refresh button takes the focus from a row with no current tab, so that row loses its light

  - test: Jest panel.spec.ts: fake timers, advance 10 s, assert one reload and no turning class; with a section list scrolled, KERNELS closed, a row selected and focused, two cards open and the ... menu open, advance 10 s, assert all kept; Galata panel.spec.ts: PUT 30 workspaces, scroll the WORKSPACES list, wait 11 s, assert its scrollTop unchanged; focus Refresh, press Enter, assert the focus stays on it
  - test-tags: UNIT, FUNCTIONAL

## Section content `LISTS`

  - mechanism: 2026-10-27T18:39:07Z @kj the panel keeps the names of terminals it shut down; _model leaves them out of every render; a load that no longer lists a name drops it
